S:decision/1 MacroCOSM is the grammar Comprise mapped, made real

S:decision/26 What enough compiles from a twin is a SHORT, written beside the text and recorded by the twin

What enough compiles from a twin is a SHORT

id
S:decision/26
type
decision
summary
One fabula, many tellings: the EMF twin, enumerated meaning fabula, is the material and a short one arrangement of it.
number
26
in
S:section/20
about
T:term/short, T:term/fabula, T:term/telling, T:term/emf, T:term/atem, T:term/by-weight
provenance
{"mode":"derived","sources":["docs/MACROCOSM_OES.md#L2536-L2540"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. What enough compiles from a twin is a SHORT, written beside the text and recorded by the twin. One fabula, many tellings: the EMF twin, enumerated meaning fabula, is the material and a short one arrangement of it. The author's text is the first arrangement and is never called a short; the atem stays outside the pair. Every compile is a new file in one of two orders, the author's or by weight, and the twin lists its shorts so the material knows its tellings.

S:decision/27 A document is enumerated in its own grammar

A document is enumerated in its own grammar

id
S:decision/27
type
decision
summary
DocOES (doc/1) takes a markdown document apart into its sections, terms, examples and decisions and joins them to the reference generated from the manifests; compiled as told, the volume gives the markdown back byte for byte.
number
27
in
S:section/20
about
T:term/docoes, T:term/reference, T:term/manifest, T:term/volume, T:term/item, T:term/atem
provenance
{"mode":"derived","sources":["docs/MACROCOSM_OES.md#L2541-L2545"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. A document is enumerated in its own grammar. DocOES (doc/1) takes a markdown document apart into its sections, terms, examples and decisions and joins them to the reference generated from the manifests; compiled as told, the volume gives the markdown back byte for byte. The markdown stays the authoring surface, the volume is built at runtime and never committed, every item carries a one-line atem made by rule, and this specification's own tables are the hand-written overlay on what is generated, so nobody edits a generated item.

  1. The manifest is a .cosm file outside every Holotype, beside this spec. A Holotype names its MiniCOSM by the declaration only and never embeds the manifest. The word manifest has two senses in Comprise: this declaration of a language, and the hash list of a saved version, a bundle or a published atom. They never meet on screen.
